│ Here you go. I’m providing both the drop-in Python script content (ready to  │
│ paste into /app/pystan_analysis.py) and quick reproducibility artifacts      │
│ (Dockerfile and a tiny Makefile) so you can choose the easiest path to run   │
│ in a Python 3.11+ environment with PyStan 3.10.0.                            │
│                                                                              │
│  1 Full drop-in Python script: /app/pystan_analysis.py Copy-paste this exact │
│    content into /app/pystan_analysis.py in a Python 3.11+ environment with   │
│    PyStan 3.10.0 installed.                                                  │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│  &quot;&quot;&quot;                                                          │
│  Python/PyStan equivalent of the provided gp_rstan.R                         │
│                                                                              │
│  What this script does:                                                      │
│  - Loads data from:                                                          │
│    - /app/train_X.csv                                                        │
│    - /app/train_y.csv                                                        │
│    - /app/test_X.csv                                                         │
│    - /app/meta_public.json                                                   │
│  - Builds an ARD squared-exponential GP kernel with jitter on the diagonal   │
│  - Uses a mean function H * beta with H = [1, x1, x2] (intercept + first     │
│  two features)                                                               │
│  - Samples from the posterior with:                                          │
│    - 4 chains, iter=2000, warmup=1000, seed=1                                │
│    - control parameters approximating the R settings                         │
│  - Extracts posterior means:                                                 │
│    - alpha, sigma                                                            │
│    - rho (length P)                                                          │
│    - beta (length 3)                                                         │
│  - Writes:                                                                   │
│    - /app/alpha_est.csv (single value)                                       │
│    - /app/sigma_est.csv (single value)                                       │
│    - /app/rho_est.csv (three values, one per row)                            │
│    - /app/beta_est.csv (three values, one per row)                           │
│  &quot;&quot;&quot;                                                          │
│                                                                              │
│  import json                                                                 │
│  import numpy as np                                                          │
│  import pandas as pd                                                         │
│                                                                              │
│  # PyStan import (3.10.0)                                                    │
│  import pystan                                                               │
│                                                                              │
│  def main():                                                                 │
│      # 1) Load data                                                          │
│      train_X = pd.read_csv(&#x27;/app/train_X.csv&#x27;)                     │
│      train_y = pd.read_csv(&#x27;/app/train_y.csv&#x27;,                     │
│  header=None).squeeze().to_numpy()                                           │
│      test_X = pd.read_csv(&#x27;/app/test_X.csv&#x27;)                       │
│      with open(&#x27;/app/meta_public.json&#x27;, &#x27;r&#x27;) as f:       │
│          meta_public = json.load(f)                                          │
│                                                                              │
│      N = train_X.shape[0]                                                    │
│      D = train_X.shape[1]                                                    │
│      N_new = test_X.shape[0] if test_X is not None else 0                    │
│                                                                              │
│      P = int(meta_public.get(&#x27;P&#x27;, min(3, D)))                      │
│      jitter = float(meta_public.get(&#x27;jitter&#x27;, 1e-6))               │
│                                                                              │
│      # 2) Prepare data for Stan                                              │
│      # X: N x P (use first P features)                                       │
│      P_eff = min(P, D)                                                       │
│      X = train_X.iloc[:, :P_eff].to_numpy()                                  │
│                                                                              │
│      # H: intercept + first two features -&gt; shape (N, 3)                  │
│      H = np.ones((N, 3))                                                     │
│      if P_eff &gt;= 1:                                                       │
│          H[:, 1] = X[:, 0]                                                   │
│      if P_eff &gt;= 2:                                                       │
│          H[:, 2] = X[:, 1]                                                   │
│                                                                              │
│      y = train_y  # shape (N,)                                               │
│                                                                              │
│      # Stan model string (ARD squared-exponential GP with jitter on          │
│  diagonal)                                                                   │
│      stan_code = &quot;&quot;&quot;                                          │
│      data {                                                                  │
│          int&lt;lower=1&gt; N;              // number of data points         │
│          int&lt;lower=1&gt; P;              // kernel input dimension (ARD   │
│  features)                                                                   │
│          matrix[N, P] X;                // input features for kernel         │
│          matrix[N, 3] H;                // mean function design matrix       │
│          vector[N] y;                   // observations                      │
│          real jitter;                   // jitter on the diagonal            │
│      }                                                                       │
│      parameters {                                                            │
│          real&lt;lower=0&gt; alpha;                                          │
│          vector&lt;lower=0&gt;[P] rho;                                       │
│          vector[3] beta;                                                     │
│          real&lt;lower=0&gt; sigma;                                          │
│      }                                                                       │
│      transformed parameters {                                                │
│          matrix[N, N] K;                                                     │
│          matrix[N, N] Ky;                                                    │
│          // ARD squared-exponential kernel                                   │
│          for (i in 1:N) {                                                    │
│              for (j in 1:N) {                                                │
│                  real dist = 0;                                              │
│                  for (p in 1:P) {                                            │
│                      real diff = X[i, p] - X[j, p];                          │
│                      dist += rho[p] * diff * diff;                           │
│                  }                                                           │
│                  K[i, j] = alpha * exp(-0.5 * dist);                         │
│              }                                                               │
│          }                                                                   │
│          // Add white noise on diagonal: sigma^2 and jitter                  │
│          Ky = K;                                                             │
│          for (n in 1:N) {                                                    │
│              Ky[n, n] = Ky[n, n] + sigma * sigma + jitter;                   │
│          }                                                                   │
│      }                                                                       │
│      model {                                                                 │
│          // Priors (mirroring R script semantics)                            │
│          alpha ~ lognormal(0, 1);                                            │
│          rho ~ lognormal(0, 1);                                              │
│          beta ~ normal(0, 5);                                                │
│          sigma ~ cauchy(0, 2);                                               │
│                                                                              │
│          // Likelihood                                                       │
│          y ~ multi_normal(H * beta, Ky);                                     │
│      }                                                                       │
│      &quot;&quot;&quot;                                                      │
│                                                                              │
│      # 3) Compile and sample                                                 │
│      model = pystan.StanModel(model_code=stan_code)                          │
│      data = {                                                                │
│          &#x27;N&#x27;: N,                                                   │
│          &#x27;P&#x27;: P_eff,                                               │
│          &#x27;X&#x27;: X,                                                   │
│          &#x27;H&#x27;: H,                                                   │
│          &#x27;y&#x27;: y,                                                   │
│          &#x27;jitter&#x27;: jitter                                          │
│      }                                                                       │
│                                                                              │
│      control = {                                                             │
│          &#x27;adapt_delta&#x27;: 0.95,                                      │
│          &#x27;max_treedepth&#x27;: 12                                       │
│      }                                                                       │
│                                                                              │
│      fit = model.sampling(data=data, iter=2000, warmup=1000, chains=4,       │
│  seed=1, control=control)                                                    │
│                                                                              │
│      # 4) Extract posteriors and compute means                               │
│      extract = fit.extract(permuted=True)                                    │
│                                                                              │
│      alpha_post = float(np.mean(extract[&#x27;alpha&#x27;]))                 │
│      sigma_post = float(np.mean(extract[&#x27;sigma&#x27;]))                 │
│      rho_post = np.mean(extract[&#x27;rho&#x27;], axis=0)  # length P_eff    │
│      beta_post = np.mean(extract[&#x27;beta&#x27;], axis=0)  # length 3      │
│                                                                              │
│      # 5) Save numeric CSVs (no headers)                                     │
│      pd.Series([alpha_post]).to_csv(&#x27;/app/alpha_est.csv&#x27;,          │
│  index=False, header=False)                                                  │
│      pd.Series([sigma_post]).to_csv(&#x27;/app/sigma_est.csv&#x27;,          │
│  index=False, header=False)                                                  │
│      pd.Series(rho_post).to_csv(&#x27;/app/rho_est.csv&#x27;, index=False,   │
│  header=False)                                                               │
│      pd.Series(beta_post).to_csv(&#x27;/app/beta_est.csv&#x27;,              │
│  index=False, header=False)                                                  │
│                                                                              │
│  if __name__ == &quot;__main__&quot;:                                        │
│      main()                                                                  │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │

│ \&#x27;X\&#x27;: X,\n        \&#x27;H\&#x27;: H,\n        \&#x27;y\&#x27;:   │
│ y,\n        \&#x27;jitter\&#x27;: jitter\n    }\n\n    control = {\n         │
│ \&#x27;adapt_delta\&#x27;: 0.95,\n        \&#x27;max_treedepth\&#x27;: 12\n  │
│ }\n\n    fit = model.sampling(data=data, iter=2000, warmup=1000, chains=4,   │
│ seed=1, control=control)\n\n    # 4) Extract posteriors and compute means\n  │
│ extract = fit.extract(permuted=True)\n\n    alpha_post =                     │
│ float(np.mean(extract[\&#x27;alpha\&#x27;]))\n    sigma_post =               │
│ float(np.mean(extract[\&#x27;sigma\&#x27;]))\n    rho_post =                 │
│ np.mean(extract[\&#x27;rho\&#x27;], axis=0)  # length P_eff\n    beta_post = │
│ np.mean(extract[\&#x27;beta\&#x27;], axis=0)  # length 3\n\n    # 5) Save    │
│ numeric CSVs (no headers)\n                                                  │
│ pd.Series([alpha_post]).to_csv(\&#x27;/app/alpha_est.csv\&#x27;,             │
│ index=False, header=False)\n                                                 │
│ pd.Series([sigma_post]).to_csv(\&#x27;/app/sigma_est.csv\&#x27;,             │
│ index=False, header=False)\n                                                 │
│ pd.Series(rho_post).to_csv(\&#x27;/app/rho_est.csv\&#x27;, index=False,      │
│ header=False)\n                                                              │
│ pd.Series(beta_post).to_csv(\&#x27;/app/beta_est.csv\&#x27;, index=False,    │
│ header=False)\n\nif __name__ == &quot;__main__&quot;:\n    main()\n\n\n2)    │
│ Ready-to-run reproducibility artifacts (optional)\n\nA) Dockerfile (Python   │
│ 3.11 + PyStan 3.10.0)\ndockerfile\nFROM python:3.11-slim\n\n# Install PyStan │
│ 3.10.0\nRUN python -m pip install --upgrade pip\nRUN python -m pip install   │
│ pystan==3.10.0\n\n# Copy application data\nCOPY /app /app\n\nWORKDIR         │
│ /app\n\n# Run the analysis script by default\nCMD [&quot;python&quot;,       │
│ &quot;pystan_analysis.py&quot;]\n\n\nB) Minimal Makefile (optional           │
│ helper)\nMakefile (optional)\n- Place in project root if you want a tiny     │
│ helper to run in an already prepared 3.11 env.\n\n.PHONY: env run            │
│ verify\n\nenv:\n\t@python3.11 -m venv venv_311\n\t@source                    │
│ venv_311/bin/activate && \\n\t  pip install --upgrade pip && \\n\t  pip      │
│ install pystan==3.10.0\n\nrun:\n\t@source venv_311/bin/activate && \\n\t     │
│ python /app/pystan_analysis.py\n\nverify:\n\t@echo "alpha:"; head -n 1       │
│ /app/alpha_est.csv\n\t@echo "sigma:"; head -n 1 /app/sigma_est.csv\n\t@echo  │

│ Solver here. Acknowledged: we cannot run PyStan 3.10.0 in this Python 3.12   │

│                                                                              │
│ What I prepared (for your use)                                               │
│                                                                              │
│  • A drop-in /app/pystan_analysis.py script (ready to paste) that mirrors    │
│    the R script’s workflow.                                                  │
│  • Ready-to-use reproducibility artifacts (optional).                        │
│                                                                              │
│  1 Full drop-in Python script: /app/pystan_analysis.py Copy-paste this exact │
│    content into /app/pystan_analysis.py in a Python 3.11+ environment with   │
│    PyStan 3.10.0 installed.                                                  │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│  &quot;&quot;&quot;                                                          │
│  Python/PyStan equivalent of the provided gp_rstan.R                         │
│                                                                              │
│  What this script does:                                                      │
│  - Loads data from:                                                          │
│    - /app/train_X.csv                                                        │
│    - /app/train_y.csv                                                        │
│    - /app/test_X.csv                                                         │
│    - /app/meta_public.json                                                   │
│  - Builds an ARD squared-exponential GP kernel with jitter on the diagonal   │
│  - Uses a mean function H * beta with H = [1, x1, x2] (intercept + first     │
│  two features)                                                               │
│  - Samples from the posterior with:                                          │
│    - 4 chains, iter=2000, warmup=1000, seed=1                                │
│    - control parameters approximating the R settings                         │
│  - Extracts posterior means:                                                 │
│    - alpha, sigma                                                            │
│    - rho (length P)                                                          │
│    - beta (length 3)                                                         │
│  - Writes:                                                                   │
│    - /app/alpha_est.csv (single value)                                       │
│    - /app/sigma_est.csv (single value)                                       │
│    - /app/rho_est.csv (three values, one per row)                            │
│    - /app/beta_est.csv (three values, one per row)                           │
│  &quot;&quot;&quot;                                                          │
│                                                                              │
│  import json                                                                 │
│  import numpy as np                                                          │
│  import pandas as pd                                                         │
│                                                                              │
│  # PyStan import (3.10.0)                                                    │
│  import pystan                                                               │
│                                                                              │
│  def main():                                                                 │
│      # 1) Load data                                                          │
│      train_X = pd.read_csv(&#x27;/app/train_X.csv&#x27;)                     │
│      train_y = pd.read_csv(&#x27;/app/train_y.csv&#x27;,                     │
│  header=None).squeeze().to_numpy()                                           │
│      test_X = pd.read_csv(&#x27;/app/test_X.csv&#x27;)                       │
│      with open(&#x27;/app/meta_public.json&#x27;, &#x27;r&#x27;) as f:       │
│          meta_public = json.load(f)                                          │
│                                                                              │
│      N = train_X.shape[0]                                                    │
│      D = train_X.shape[1]                                                    │
│      N_new = test_X.shape[0] if test_X is not None else 0                    │
│                                                                              │
│      P = int(meta_public.get(&#x27;P&#x27;, min(3, D)))                      │
│      jitter = float(meta_public.get(&#x27;jitter&#x27;, 1e-6))               │
│                                                                              │
│      # 2) Prepare data for Stan                                              │
│      # X: N x P (use first P features)                                       │
│      P_eff = min(P, D)                                                       │
│      X = train_X.iloc[:, :P_eff].to_numpy()                                  │
│                                                                              │
│      # H: intercept + first two features -&gt; shape (N, 3)                  │
│      H = np.ones((N, 3))                                                     │
│      if P_eff &gt;= 1:                                                       │
│          H[:, 1] = X[:, 0]                                                   │
│      if P_eff &gt;= 2:                                                       │
│          H[:, 2] = X[:, 1]                                                   │
│                                                                              │
│      y = train_y  # shape (N,)                                               │
│                                                                              │
│      # Stan model string (ARD squared-exponential GP with jitter on          │
│  diagonal)                                                                   │
│      stan_code = &quot;&quot;&quot;                                          │
│      data {                                                                  │
│          int&lt;lower=1&gt; N;              // number of data points         │
│          int&lt;lower=1&gt; P;              // kernel input dimension (ARD   │
│  features)                                                                   │
│          matrix[N, P] X;                // input features for kernel         │
│          matrix[N, 3] H;                // mean function design matrix       │
│          vector[N] y;                   // observations                      │
│          real jitter;                   // jitter on the diagonal            │
│      }                                                                       │
│      parameters {                                                            │
│          real&lt;lower=0&gt; alpha;                                          │
│          vector&lt;lower=0&gt;[P] rho;                                       │
│          vector[3] beta;                                                     │
│          real&lt;lower=0&gt; sigma;                                          │
│      }                                                                       │
│      transformed parameters {                                                │
│          matrix[N, N] K;                                                     │
│          matrix[N, N] Ky;                                                    │
│          // ARD squared-exponential kernel                                   │
│          for (i in 1:N) {                                                    │
│              for (j in 1:N) {                                                │
│                  real dist = 0;                                              │
│                  for (p in 1:P) {                                            │
│                      real diff = X[i, p] - X[j, p];                          │
│                      dist += rho[p] * diff * diff;                           │
│                  }                                                           │
│                  K[i, j] = alpha * exp(-0.5 * dist);                         │
│              }                                                               │
│          }                                                                   │
│          // Add white noise on diagonal: sigma^2 and jitter                  │
│          Ky = K;                                                             │
│          for (n in 1:N) {                                                    │
│              Ky[n, n] = Ky[n, n] + sigma * sigma + jitter;                   │
│          }                                                                   │
│      }                                                                       │
│      model {                                                                 │
│          // Priors (mirroring R script semantics)                            │
│          alpha ~ lognormal(0, 1);                                            │
│          rho ~ lognormal(0, 1);                                              │
│          beta ~ normal(0, 5);                                                │
│          sigma ~ cauchy(0, 2);                                               │
│                                                                              │
│          // Likelihood                                                       │
│          y ~ multi_normal(H * beta, Ky);                                     │
│      }                                                                       │
│      &quot;&quot;&quot;                                                      │
│                                                                              │
│      # 3) Compile and sample                                                 │
│      model = pystan.StanModel(model_code=stan_code)                          │
│      data = {                                                                │
│          &#x27;N&#x27;: N,                                                   │
│          &#x27;P&#x27;: P_eff,                                               │
│          &#x27;X&#x27;: X,                                                   │
│          &#x27;H&#x27;: H,                                                   │
│          &#x27;y&#x27;: y,                                                   │
│          &#x27;jitter&#x27;: jitter                                          │
│      }                                                                       │
│                                                                              │
│      control = {                                                             │
│          &#x27;adapt_delta&#x27;: 0.95,                                      │
│          &#x27;max_treedepth&#x27;: 12                                       │
│      }                                                                       │
│                                                                              │
│      fit = model.sampling(data=data, iter=2000, warmup=1000, chains=4,       │
│  seed=1, control=control)                                                    │
│                                                                              │
│      # 4) Extract posteriors and compute means                               │
│      extract = fit.extract(permuted=True)                                    │
│                                                                              │
│      alpha_post = float(np.mean(extract[&#x27;alpha&#x27;]))                 │
│      sigma_post = float(np.mean(extract[&#x27;sigma&#x27;]))                 │
│      rho_post = np.mean(extract[&#x27;rho&#x27;], axis=0)  # length P_eff    │
│      beta_post = np.mean(extract[&#x27;beta&#x27;], axis=0)  # length 3      │
│                                                                              │
│      # 5) Save numeric CSVs (no headers)                                     │
│      pd.Series([alpha_post]).to_csv(&#x27;/app/alpha_est.csv&#x27;,          │
│  index=False, header=False)                                                  │
│      pd.Series([sigma_post]).to_csv(&#x27;/app/sigma_est.csv&#x27;,          │
│  index=False, header=False)                                                  │
│      pd.Series(rho_post).to_csv(&#x27;/app/rho_est.csv&#x27;, index=False,   │
│  header=False)                                                               │
│      pd.Series(beta_post).to_csv(&#x27;/app/beta_est.csv&#x27;,              │
│  index=False, header=False)                                                  │
│                                                                              │
│  if __name__ == &quot;__main__&quot;:                                        │
│      main()                                                                  │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │

│ train_y = pd.read_csv(\&#x27;/app/train_y.csv\&#x27;,                        │
│ header=None).squeeze().to_numpy()\n    test_X =                              │
│ pd.read_csv(\&#x27;/app/test_X.csv\&#x27;)\n    with                         │
│ open(\&#x27;/app/meta_public.json\&#x27;, \&#x27;r\&#x27;) as f:\n           │
│ meta_public = json.load(f)\n\n    N = train_X.shape[0]\n    D =              │
│ train_X.shape[1]\n    N_new = test_X.shape[0] if test_X is not None else     │
│ 0\n\n    P = int(meta_public.get(\&#x27;P\&#x27;, min(3, D)))\n    jitter =  │
│ float(meta_public.get(\&#x27;jitter\&#x27;, 1e-6))\n\n    # 2) Prepare data  │
│ for Stan\n    # X: N x P (use first P features)\n    P_eff = min(P, D)\n     │
│ X = train_X.iloc[:, :P_eff].to_numpy()\n\n    # H: intercept + first two     │
│ features -&gt; shape (N, 3)\n    H = np.ones((N, 3))\n    if P_eff &gt;=     │
│ 1:\n        H[:, 1] = X[:, 0]\n    if P_eff &gt;= 2:\n        H[:, 2] = X[:, │
│ 1]\n\n    y = train_y  # shape (N,)\n\n    # Stan model string (ARD          │
│ squared-exponential GP with jitter on diagonal)\n    stan_code =             │
│ &quot;&quot;&quot;\n    data {\n        int&lt;lower=1&gt; N;                │
│ // number of data points\n        int&lt;lower=1&gt; P;              //      │
│ kernel input dimension (ARD features)\n        matrix[N, P] X;               │
│ // input features for kernel\n        matrix[N, 3] H;                // mean │
│ function design matrix\n        vector[N] y;                   //            │
│ observations\n        real jitter;                   // jitter on the        │
│ diagonal\n    }\n    parameters {\n        real&lt;lower=0&gt; alpha;\n      │
│ vector&lt;lower=0&gt;[P] rho;\n        vector[3] beta;\n                     │
│ real&lt;lower=0&gt; sigma;\n    }\n    transformed parameters {\n            │
│ matrix[N, N] K;\n        matrix[N, N] Ky;\n        // ARD                    │
│ squared-exponential kernel\n        for (i in 1:N) {\n            for (j in  │
│ 1:N) {\n                real dist = 0;\n                for (p in 1:P) {\n   │
│ real diff = X[i, p] - X[j, p];\n                    dist += rho[p] * diff *  │
│ diff;\n                }\n                K[i, j] = alpha * exp(-0.5 *       │
│ dist);\n            }\n        }\n        // Add white noise on diagonal:    │
│ sigma^2 and jitter\n        Ky = K;\n        for (n in 1:N) {\n              │
│ Ky[n, n] = Ky[n, n] + sigma * sigma + jitter;\n        }\n    }\n    model   │
│ {\n        // Priors (mirroring R script semantics)\n        alpha ~         │
│ lognormal(0, 1);\n        rho ~ lognormal(0, 1);\n        beta ~ normal(0,   │
│ 5);\n        sigma ~ cauchy(0, 2);\n\n        // Likelihood\n        y ~     │
│ multi_normal(H * beta, Ky);\n    }\n    &quot;&quot;&quot;\n\n    # 3)       │
│ Compile and sample\n    model = pystan.StanModel(model_code=stan_code)\n     │
│ data = {\n        \&#x27;N\&#x27;: N,\n        \&#x27;P\&#x27;: P_eff,\n     │
│ \&#x27;X\&#x27;: X,\n        \&#x27;H\&#x27;: H,\n        \&#x27;y\&#x27;:   │
│ y,\n        \&#x27;jitter\&#x27;: jitter\n    }\n\n    control = {\n         │
│ \&#x27;adapt_delta\&#x27;: 0.95,\n        \&#x27;max_treedepth\&#x27;: 12\n  │
│ }\n\n    fit = model.sampling(data=data, iter=2000, warmup=1000, chains=4,   │
│ seed=1, control=control)\n\n    # 4) Extract posteriors and compute means\n  │
│ extract = fit.extract(permuted=True)\n\n    alpha_post =                     │
│ float(np.mean(extract[\&#x27;alpha\&#x27;]))\n    sigma_post =               │
│ float(np.mean(extract[\&#x27;sigma\&#x27;]))\n    rho_post =                 │
│ np.mean(extract[\&#x27;rho\&#x27;], axis=0)  # length P_eff\n    beta_post = │
│ np.mean(extract[\&#x27;beta\&#x27;], axis=0)  # length 3\n\n    # 5) Save    │
│ numeric CSVs (no headers)\n                                                  │
│ pd.Series([alpha_post]).to_csv(\&#x27;/app/alpha_est.csv\&#x27;,             │
│ index=False, header=False)\n                                                 │
│ pd.Series([sigma_post]).to_csv(\&#x27;/app/sigma_est.csv\&#x27;,             │
│ index=False, header=False)\n                                                 │
│ pd.Series(rho_post).to_csv(\&#x27;/app/rho_est.csv\&#x27;, index=False,      │
│ header=False)\n                                                              │
│ pd.Series(beta_post).to_csv(\&#x27;/app/beta_est.csv\&#x27;, index=False,    │
│ header=False)\n\nif __name__ == &quot;__main__&quot;:\n    main()\n\n\n2)    │

╭────────────────────────────── solver - message ──────────────────────────────╮
│ Acknowledged. Here’s a concise, ready-to-run package for finishing the task  │
│ in a Python 3.11+ environment with PyStan 3.10.0. I’m including the drop-in  │
│ Python script, a minimal Dockerfile, a tiny Makefile (optional), and a       │
│ GitHub Actions workflow snippet to automate the process. The plan assumes    │
│ you select Option A (virtualenv in Python 3.11), but the artifacts below     │
│ also cover containerized and CI-based execution.                             │
│                                                                              │
│  1 /app/pystan_analysis.py (drop-in Python 3.11+ script) Copy-paste into     │
│    /app/pystan_analysis.py in a Python 3.11+ environment with PyStan 3.10.0  │
│    installed.                                                                │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│  &quot;&quot;&quot;                                                          │
│  Python/PyStan equivalent of the provided gp_rstan.R                         │
│                                                                              │
│  What this script does:                                                      │
│  - Loads data from:                                                          │
│    - /app/train_X.csv                                                        │
│    - /app/train_y.csv                                                        │
│    - /app/test_X.csv                                                         │
│    - /app/meta_public.json                                                   │
│  - Builds an ARD squared-exponential GP kernel with jitter on the diagonal   │
│  - Uses a mean function H * beta with H = [1, x1, x2] (intercept + first     │
│  two features)                                                               │
│  - Samples from the posterior with:                                          │
│    - 4 chains, iter=2000, warmup=1000, seed=1                                │
│    - control parameters approximating the R settings                         │
│  - Extracts posterior means:                                                 │
│    - alpha, sigma                                                            │
│    - rho (length P)                                                          │
│    - beta (length 3)                                                         │
│  - Writes:                                                                   │
│    - /app/alpha_est.csv (single value)                                       │
│    - /app/sigma_est.csv (single value)                                       │
│    - /app/rho_est.csv (three values, one per row)                            │
│    - /app/beta_est.csv (three values, one per row)                           │
│  &quot;&quot;&quot;                                                          │
│                                                                              │
│  import json                                                                 │
│  import numpy as np                                                          │
│  import pandas as pd                                                         │
│                                                                              │
│  # PyStan import (3.10.0)                                                    │
│  import pystan                                                               │
│                                                                              │
│  def main():                                                                 │
│      # 1) Load data                                                          │
│      train_X = pd.read_csv(&#x27;/app/train_X.csv&#x27;)                     │
│      train_y = pd.read_csv(&#x27;/app/train_y.csv&#x27;,                     │
│  header=None).squeeze().to_numpy()                                           │
│      test_X = pd.read_csv(&#x27;/app/test_X.csv&#x27;)                       │
│      with open(&#x27;/app/meta_public.json&#x27;, &#x27;r&#x27;) as f:       │
│          meta_public = json.load(f)                                          │
│                                                                              │
│      N = train_X.shape[0]                                                    │
│      D = train_X.shape[1]                                                    │
│      N_new = test_X.shape[0] if test_X is not None else 0                    │
│                                                                              │
│      P = int(meta_public.get(&#x27;P&#x27;, min(3, D)))                      │
│      jitter = float(meta_public.get(&#x27;jitter&#x27;, 1e-6))               │
│                                                                              │
│      # 2) Prepare data for Stan                                              │
│      # X: N x P (use first P features)                                       │
│      P_eff = min(P, D)                                                       │
│      X = train_X.iloc[:, :P_eff].to_numpy()                                  │
│                                                                              │
│      # H: intercept + first two features -&gt; shape (N, 3)                  │
│      H = np.ones((N, 3))                                                     │
│      if P_eff &gt;= 1:                                                       │
│          H[:, 1] = X[:, 0]                                                   │
│      if P_eff &gt;= 2:                                                       │
│          H[:, 2] = X[:, 1]                                                   │
│                                                                              │
│      y = train_y  # shape (N,)                                               │
│                                                                              │
│      # Stan model string (ARD squared-exponential GP with jitter on          │
│  diagonal)                                                                   │
│      stan_code = &quot;&quot;&quot;                                          │
│      data {                                                                  │
│          int&lt;lower=1&gt; N;              // number of data points         │
│          int&lt;lower=1&gt; P;              // kernel input dimension (ARD   │
│  features)                                                                   │
│          matrix[N, P] X;                // input features for kernel         │
│          matrix[N, 3] H;                // mean function design matrix       │
│          vector[N] y;                   // observations                      │
│          real jitter;                   // jitter on the diagonal            │
│      }                                                                       │
│      parameters {                                                            │
│          real&lt;lower=0&gt; alpha;                                          │
│          vector&lt;lower=0&gt;[P] rho;                                       │
│          vector[3] beta;                                                     │
│          real&lt;lower=0&gt; sigma;                                          │
│      }                                                                       │
│      transformed parameters {                                                │
│          matrix[N, N] K;                                                     │
│          matrix[N, N] Ky;                                                    │
│          // ARD squared-exponential kernel                                   │
│          for (i in 1:N) {                                                    │
│              for (j in 1:N) {                                                │
│                  real dist = 0;                                              │
│                  for (p in 1:P) {                                            │
│                      real diff = X[i, p] - X[j, p];                          │
│                      dist += rho[p] * diff * diff;                           │
│                  }                                                           │
│                  K[i, j] = alpha * exp(-0.5 * dist);                         │
│              }                                                               │
│          }                                                                   │
│          // Add white noise on diagonal: sigma^2 and jitter                  │
│          Ky = K;                                                             │
│          for (n in 1:N) {                                                    │
│              Ky[n, n] = Ky[n, n] + sigma * sigma + jitter;                   │
│          }                                                                   │
│      }                                                                       │
│      model {                                                                 │
│          // Priors (mirroring R script semantics)                            │
│          alpha ~ lognormal(0, 1);                                            │
│          rho ~ lognormal(0, 1);                                              │
│          beta ~ normal(0, 5);                                                │
│          sigma ~ cauchy(0, 2);                                               │
│                                                                              │
│          // Likelihood                                                       │
│          y ~ multi_normal(H * beta, Ky);                                     │
│      }                                                                       │
│      &quot;&quot;&quot;                                                      │
│                                                                              │
│      # 3) Compile and sample                                                 │
│      model = pystan.StanModel(model_code=stan_code)                          │
│      data = {                                                                │
│          &#x27;N&#x27;: N,                                                   │
│          &#x27;P&#x27;: P_eff,                                               │
│          &#x27;X&#x27;: X,                                                   │
│          &#x27;H&#x27;: H,                                                   │
│          &#x27;y&#x27;: y,                                                   │
│          &#x27;jitter&#x27;: jitter                                          │
│      }                                                                       │
│                                                                              │
│      control = {                                                             │
│          &#x27;adapt_delta&#x27;: 0.95,                                      │
│          &#x27;max_treedepth&#x27;: 12                                       │
│      }                                                                       │
│                                                                              │
│      fit = model.sampling(data=data, iter=2000, warmup=1000, chains=4,       │
│  seed=1, control=control)                                                    │
│                                                                              │
│      # 4) Extract posteriors and compute means                               │
│      extract = fit.extract(permuted=True)                                    │
│                                                                              │
│      alpha_post = float(np.mean(extract[&#x27;alpha&#x27;]))                 │
│      sigma_post = float(np.mean(extract[&#x27;sigma&#x27;]))                 │
│      rho_post = np.mean(extract[&#x27;rho&#x27;], axis=0)  # length P_eff    │
│      beta_post = np.mean(extract[&#x27;beta&#x27;], axis=0)  # length 3      │
│                                                                              │
│      # 5) Save numeric CSVs (no headers)                                     │
│      pd.Series([alpha_post]).to_csv(&#x27;/app/alpha_est.csv&#x27;,          │
│  index=False, header=False)                                                  │
│      pd.Series([sigma_post]).to_csv(&#x27;/app/sigma_est.csv&#x27;,          │
│  index=False, header=False)                                                  │
│      pd.Series(rho_post).to_csv(&#x27;/app/rho_est.csv&#x27;, index=False,   │
│  header=False)                                                               │
│      pd.Series(beta_post).to_csv(&#x27;/app/beta_est.csv&#x27;,              │
│  index=False, header=False)                                                  │
│                                                                              │
│  if __name__ == &quot;__main__&quot;:                                        │
│      main()                                                                  │
│                                                                              │

│ script)\nCopy-paste into /app/pystan_analysis.py in a Python 3.11+           │
│ environment with PyStan 3.10.0                                               │
│ installed.\n\npython\n&quot;&quot;&quot;\nPython/PyStan equivalent of the    │
│ provided gp_rstan.R\n\nWhat this script does:\n- Loads data from:\n  -       │
│ /app/train_X.csv\n  - /app/train_y.csv\n  - /app/test_X.csv\n  -             │
│ /app/meta_public.json\n- Builds an ARD squared-exponential GP kernel with    │
│ jitter on the diagonal\n- Uses a mean function H * beta with H = [1, x1, x2] │
│ (intercept + first two features)\n- Samples from the posterior with:\n  - 4  │
│ chains, iter=2000, warmup=1000, seed=1\n  - control parameters approximating │
│ the R settings\n- Extracts posterior means:\n  - alpha, sigma\n  - rho       │
│ (length P)\n  - beta (length 3)\n- Writes:\n  - /app/alpha_est.csv (single   │
│ value)\n  - /app/sigma_est.csv (single value)\n  - /app/rho_est.csv (three   │
│ values, one per row)\n  - /app/beta_est.csv (three values, one per           │
│ row)\n&quot;&quot;&quot;\n\nimport json\nimport numpy as np\nimport pandas   │
│ as pd\n\n# PyStan import (3.10.0)\nimport pystan\n\ndef main():\n    # 1)    │
│ Load data\n    train_X = pd.read_csv(\&#x27;/app/train_X.csv\&#x27;)\n       │
│ train_y = pd.read_csv(\&#x27;/app/train_y.csv\&#x27;,                        │
│ header=None).squeeze().to_numpy()\n    test_X =                              │
│ pd.read_csv(\&#x27;/app/test_X.csv\&#x27;)\n    with                         │
│ open(\&#x27;/app/meta_public.json\&#x27;, \&#x27;r\&#x27;) as f:\n           │
│ meta_public = json.load(f)\n\n    N = train_X.shape[0]\n    D =              │
│ train_X.shape[1]\n    N_new = test_X.shape[0] if test_X is not None else     │
│ 0\n\n    P = int(meta_public.get(\&#x27;P\&#x27;, min(3, D)))\n    jitter =  │
│ float(meta_public.get(\&#x27;jitter\&#x27;, 1e-6))\n\n    # 2) Prepare data  │
│ for Stan\n    # X: N x P (use first P features)\n    P_eff = min(P, D)\n     │
│ X = train_X.iloc[:, :P_eff].to_numpy()\n\n    # H: intercept + first two     │
│ features -&gt; shape (N, 3)\n    H = np.ones((N, 3))\n    if P_eff &gt;=     │
│ 1:\n        H[:, 1] = X[:, 0]\n    if P_eff &gt;= 2:\n        H[:, 2] = X[:, │
│ 1]\n\n    y = train_y  # shape (N,)\n\n    # Stan model string (ARD          │
│ squared-exponential GP with jitter on diagonal)\n    stan_code =             │
│ &quot;&quot;&quot;\n    data {\n        int&lt;lower=1&gt; N;                │
│ // number of data points\n        int&lt;lower=1&gt; P;              //      │
│ kernel input dimension (ARD features)\n        matrix[N, P] X;               │
│ // input features for kernel\n        matrix[N, 3] H;                // mean │
│ function design matrix\n        vector[N] y;                   //            │
│ observations\n        real jitter;                   // jitter on the        │
│ diagonal\n    }\n    parameters {\n        real&lt;lower=0&gt; alpha;\n      │
│ vector&lt;lower=0&gt;[P] rho;\n        vector[3] beta;\n                     │
│ real&lt;lower=0&gt; sigma;\n    }\n    transformed parameters {\n            │
│ matrix[N, N] K;\n        matrix[N, N] Ky;\n        // ARD                    │
│ squared-exponential kernel\n        for (i in 1:N) {\n            for (j in  │
│ 1:N) {\n                real dist = 0;\n                for (p in 1:P) {\n   │
│ real diff = X[i, p] - X[j, p];\n                    dist += rho[p] * diff *  │
│ diff;\n                }\n                K[i, j] = alpha * exp(-0.5 *       │
│ dist);\n            }\n        }\n        // Add white noise on diagonal:    │
│ sigma^2 and jitter\n        Ky = K;\n        for (n in 1:N) {\n              │
│ Ky[n, n] = Ky[n, n] + sigma * sigma + jitter;\n        }\n    }\n    model   │
│ {\n        // Priors (mirroring R script semantics)\n        alpha ~         │
│ lognormal(0, 1);\n        rho ~ lognormal(0, 1);\n        beta ~ normal(0,   │
│ 5);\n        sigma ~ cauchy(0, 2);\n\n        // Likelihood\n        y ~     │
│ multi_normal(H * beta, Ky);\n    }\n    &quot;&quot;&quot;\n\n    # 3)       │
│ Compile and sample\n    model = pystan.StanModel(model_code=stan_code)\n     │
│ data = {\n        \&#x27;N\&#x27;: N,\n        \&#x27;P\&#x27;: P_eff,\n     │
│ \&#x27;X\&#x27;: X,\n        \&#x27;H\&#x27;: H,\n        \&#x27;y\&#x27;:   │
│ y,\n        \&#x27;jitter\&#x27;: jitter\n    }\n\n    control = {\n         │
│ \&#x27;adapt_delta\&#x27;: 0.95,\n        \&#x27;max_treedepth\&#x27;: 12\n  │
│ }\n\n    fit = model.sampling(data=data, iter=2000, warmup=1000, chains=4,   │
│ seed=1, control=control)\n\n    # 4) Extract posteriors and compute means\n  │
│ extract = fit.extract(permuted=True)\n\n    alpha_post =                     │
│ float(np.mean(extract[\&#x27;alpha\&#x27;]))\n    sigma_post =               │
│ float(np.mean(extract[\&#x27;sigma\&#x27;]))\n    rho_post =                 │
│ np.mean(extract[\&#x27;rho\&#x27;], axis=0)  # length P_eff\n    beta_post = │
│ np.mean(extract[\&#x27;beta\&#x27;], axis=0)  # length 3\n\n    # 5) Save    │
│ numeric CSVs (no headers)\n                                                  │
│ pd.Series([alpha_post]).to_csv(\&#x27;/app/alpha_est.csv\&#x27;,             │
│ index=False, header=False)\n                                                 │
│ pd.Series([sigma_post]).to_csv(\&#x27;/app/sigma_est.csv\&#x27;,             │
│ index=False, header=False)\n                                                 │
│ pd.Series(rho_post).to_csv(\&#x27;/app/rho_est.csv\&#x27;, index=False,      │
│ header=False)\n                                                              │
│ pd.Series(beta_post).to_csv(\&#x27;/app/beta_est.csv\&#x27;, index=False,    │
│ header=False)\n\nif __name__ == &quot;__main__&quot;:\n    main()\n\n\n2)    │

│      D = train_X.shape[1]                                                    │
│      N_new = test_X.shape[0] if test_X is not None else 0                    │
│                                                                              │
│      P = int(meta_public.get(&#x27;P&#x27;, min(3, D)))                      │
│      jitter = float(meta_public.get(&#x27;jitter&#x27;, 1e-6))               │
│                                                                              │
│      # 2) Prepare data for Stan                                              │
│      # X: N x P (use first P features)                                       │
│      P_eff = min(P, D)                                                       │
│      X = train_X.iloc[:, :P_eff].to_numpy()                                  │
│                                                                              │
│      # H: intercept + first two features -&gt; shape (N, 3)                  │
│      H = np.ones((N, 3))                                                     │
│      if P_eff &gt;= 1:                                                       │
│          H[:, 1] = X[:, 0]                                                   │
│      if P_eff &gt;= 2:                                                       │
│          H[:, 2] = X[:, 1]                                                   │
│                                                                              │
│      y = train_y  # shape (N,)                                               │
│                                                                              │
│      # Stan model string (ARD squared-exponential GP with jitter on          │
│  diagonal)                                                                   │
│      stan_code = &quot;&quot;&quot;                                          │
│      data {                                                                  │
│          int&lt;lower=1&gt; N;              // number of data points         │
│          int&lt;lower=1&gt; P;              // kernel input dimension (ARD   │
│  features)                                                                   │
│          matrix[N, P] X;                // input features for kernel         │
│          matrix[N, 3] H;                // mean function design matrix       │
│          vector[N] y;                   // observations                      │
│          real jitter;                   // jitter on the diagonal            │
│      }                                                                       │
│      parameters {                                                            │
│          real&lt;lower=0&gt; alpha;                                          │
│          vector&lt;lower=0&gt;[P] rho;                                       │
│          vector[3] beta;                                                     │
│          real&lt;lower=0&gt; sigma;                                          │
│      }                                                                       │
│      transformed parameters {                                                │
│          matrix[N, N] K;                                                     │
│          matrix[N, N] Ky;                                                    │
│          // ARD squared-exponential kernel                                   │
│          for (i in 1:N) {                                                    │
│              for (j in 1:N) {                                                │
│                  real dist = 0;                                              │
│                  for (p in 1:P) {                                            │
│                      real diff = X[i, p] - X[j, p];                          │
│                      dist += rho[p] * diff * diff;                           │
│                  }                                                           │
│                  K[i, j] = alpha * exp(-0.5 * dist);                         │
│              }                                                               │
│          }                                                                   │
│          // Add white noise on diagonal: sigma^2 and jitter                  │
│          Ky = K;                                                             │
│          for (n in 1:N) {                                                    │
│              Ky[n, n] = Ky[n, n] + sigma * sigma + jitter;                   │
│          }                                                                   │
│      }                                                                       │
│      model {                                                                 │
│          // Priors (mirroring R script semantics)                            │
│          alpha ~ lognormal(0, 1);                                            │
│          rho ~ lognormal(0, 1);                                              │
│          beta ~ normal(0, 5);                                                │
│          sigma ~ cauchy(0, 2);                                               │
│                                                                              │
│          // Likelihood                                                       │
│          y ~ multi_normal(H * beta, Ky);                                     │
│      }                                                                       │
│      &quot;&quot;&quot;                                                      │
│                                                                              │
│      # 3) Compile and sample                                                 │
│      model = pystan.StanModel(model_code=stan_code)                          │
│      data = {                                                                │
│          &#x27;N&#x27;: N,                                                   │
│          &#x27;P&#x27;: P_eff,                                               │
│          &#x27;X&#x27;: X,                                                   │
│          &#x27;H&#x27;: H,                                                   │
│          &#x27;y&#x27;: y,                                                   │
│          &#x27;jitter&#x27;: jitter                                          │
│      }                                                                       │
│                                                                              │
│      control = {                                                             │
│          &#x27;adapt_delta&#x27;: 0.95,                                      │
│          &#x27;max_treedepth&#x27;: 12                                       │
│      }                                                                       │
│                                                                              │
│      fit = model.sampling(data=data, iter=2000, warmup=1000, chains=4,       │
│  seed=1, control=control)                                                    │
│                                                                              │
│      # 4) Extract posteriors and compute means                               │
│      extract = fit.extract(permuted=True)                                    │
│                                                                              │
│      alpha_post = float(np.mean(extract[&#x27;alpha&#x27;]))                 │
│      sigma_post = float(np.mean(extract[&#x27;sigma&#x27;]))                 │
│      rho_post = np.mean(extract[&#x27;rho&#x27;], axis=0)  # length P_eff    │
│      beta_post = np.mean(extract[&#x27;beta&#x27;], axis=0)  # length 3      │
│                                                                              │
│      # 5) Save numeric CSVs (no headers)                                     │
│      pd.Series([alpha_post]).to_csv(&#x27;/app/alpha_est.csv&#x27;,          │
│  index=False, header=False)                                                  │
│      pd.Series([sigma_post]).to_csv(&#x27;/app/sigma_est.csv&#x27;,          │
│  index=False, header=False)                                                  │
│      pd.Series(rho_post).to_csv(&#x27;/app/rho_est.csv&#x27;, index=False,   │
│  header=False)                                                               │
│      pd.Series(beta_post).to_csv(&#x27;/app/beta_est.csv&#x27;,              │
│  index=False, header=False)                                                  │
│                                                                              │
│  if __name__ == &quot;__main__&quot;:                                        │
│      main()                                                                  │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │

│ jitter on the diagonal\n- Uses a mean function H * beta with H = [1, x1, x2] │

│ row)\n&quot;&quot;&quot;\n\nimport json\nimport numpy as np\nimport pandas   │
│ as pd\n\n# PyStan import (3.10.0)\nimport pystan\n\ndef main():\n    # 1)    │
│ Load data\n    train_X = pd.read_csv(\&#x27;/app/train_X.csv\&#x27;)\n       │
│ train_y = pd.read_csv(\&#x27;/app/train_y.csv\&#x27;,                        │
│ header=None).squeeze().to_numpy()\n    test_X =                              │
│ pd.read_csv(\&#x27;/app/test_X.csv\&#x27;)\n    with                         │
│ open(\&#x27;/app/meta_public.json\&#x27;, \&#x27;r\&#x27;) as f:\n           │
│ meta_public = json.load(f)\n\n    N = train_X.shape[0]\n    D =              │
│ train_X.shape[1]\n    N_new = test_X.shape[0] if test_X is not None else     │
│ 0\n\n    P = int(meta_public.get(\&#x27;P\&#x27;, min(3, D)))\n    jitter =  │
│ float(meta_public.get(\&#x27;jitter\&#x27;, 1e-6))\n\n    # 2) Prepare data  │
│ for Stan\n    # X: N x P (use first P features)\n    P_eff = min(P, D)\n     │

│ features -&gt; shape (N, 3)\n    H = np.ones((N, 3))\n    if P_eff &gt;=     │
│ 1:\n        H[:, 1] = X[:, 0]\n    if P_eff &gt;= 2:\n        H[:, 2] = X[:, │
│ 1]\n\n    y = train_y  # shape (N,)\n\n    # Stan model string (ARD          │
│ squared-exponential GP with jitter on diagonal)\n    stan_code =             │
│ &quot;&quot;&quot;\n    data {\n        int&lt;lower=1&gt; N;                │
│ // number of data points\n        int&lt;lower=1&gt; P;              //      │
│ kernel input dimension (ARD features)\n        matrix[N, P] X;               │
│ // input features for kernel\n        matrix[N, 3] H;                // mean │
│ function design matrix\n        vector[N] y;                   //            │
│ observations\n        real jitter;                   // jitter on the        │
│ diagonal\n    }\n    parameters {\n        real&lt;lower=0&gt; alpha;\n      │
│ vector&lt;lower=0&gt;[P] rho;\n        vector[3] beta;\n                     │
│ real&lt;lower=0&gt; sigma;\n    }\n    transformed parameters {\n            │
│ matrix[N, N] K;\n        matrix[N, N] Ky;\n        // ARD                    │
│ squared-exponential kernel\n        for (i in 1:N) {\n            for (j in  │
│ 1:N) {\n                real dist = 0;\n                for (p in 1:P) {\n   │
│ real diff = X[i, p] - X[j, p];\n                    dist += rho[p] * diff *  │
│ diff;\n                }\n                K[i, j] = alpha * exp(-0.5 *       │
│ dist);\n            }\n        }\n        // Add white noise on diagonal:    │
│ sigma^2 and jitter\n        Ky = K;\n        for (n in 1:N) {\n              │
│ Ky[n, n] = Ky[n, n] + sigma * sigma + jitter;\n        }\n    }\n    model   │
│ {\n        // Priors (mirroring R script semantics)\n        alpha ~         │
│ lognormal(0, 1);\n        rho ~ lognormal(0, 1);\n        beta ~ normal(0,   │
│ 5);\n        sigma ~ cauchy(0, 2);\n\n        // Likelihood\n        y ~     │
│ multi_normal(H * beta, Ky);\n    }\n    &quot;&quot;&quot;\n\n    # 3)       │
│ Compile and sample\n    model = pystan.StanModel(model_code=stan_code)\n     │
│ data = {\n        \&#x27;N\&#x27;: N,\n        \&#x27;P\&#x27;: P_eff,\n     │
│ \&#x27;X\&#x27;: X,\n        \&#x27;H\&#x27;: H,\n        \&#x27;y\&#x27;:   │
│ y,\n        \&#x27;jitter\&#x27;: jitter\n    }\n\n    control = {\n         │
│ \&#x27;adapt_delta\&#x27;: 0.95,\n        \&#x27;max_treedepth\&#x27;: 12\n  │
│ }\n\n    fit = model.sampling(data=data, iter=2000, warmup=1000, chains=4,   │
│ seed=1, control=control)\n\n    # 4) Extract posteriors and compute means\n  │
│ extract = fit.extract(permuted=True)\n\n    alpha_post =                     │
│ float(np.mean(extract[\&#x27;alpha\&#x27;]))\n    sigma_post =               │
│ float(np.mean(extract[\&#x27;sigma\&#x27;]))\n    rho_post =                 │
│ np.mean(extract[\&#x27;rho\&#x27;], axis=0)  # length P_eff\n    beta_post = │
│ np.mean(extract[\&#x27;beta\&#x27;], axis=0)  # length 3\n\n    # 5) Save    │
│ numeric CSVs (no headers)\n                                                  │
│ pd.Series([alpha_post]).to_csv(\&#x27;/app/alpha_est.csv\&#x27;,             │
│ index=False, header=False)\n                                                 │
│ pd.Series([sigma_post]).to_csv(\&#x27;/app/sigma_est.csv\&#x27;,             │
│ index=False, header=False)\n                                                 │

│ header=False)\n                                                              │

│  Python/PyStan equivalent of the provided gp_rstan.R                         │
│                                                                              │
│  What this script does:                                                      │
│  - Loads data from:                                                          │
│    - /app/train_X.csv                                                        │
│    - /app/train_y.csv                                                        │
│    - /app/test_X.csv                                                         │
│    - /app/meta_public.json                                                   │
│  - Builds an ARD squared-exponential GP kernel with jitter on the diagonal   │
│  - Uses a mean function H * beta with H = [1, x1, x2] (intercept + first     │
│  two features)                                                               │
│  - Samples from the posterior with:                                          │
│    - 4 chains, iter=2000, warmup=1000, seed=1                                │
│    - control parameters approximating the R settings                         │
│  - Extracts posterior means:                                                 │
│    - alpha, sigma                                                            │
│    - rho (length P)                                                          │
│    - beta (length 3)                                                         │
│  - Writes:                                                                   │
│    - /app/alpha_est.csv (single value)                                       │
│    - /app/sigma_est.csv (single value)                                       │
│    - /app/rho_est.csv (three values, one per row)                            │
│    - /app/beta_est.csv (three values, one per row)                           │
│  &quot;&quot;&quot;                                                          │
│                                                                              │
│  import json                                                                 │
│  import numpy as np                                                          │
│  import pandas as pd                                                         │
│                                                                              │
│  # PyStan import (3.10.0)                                                    │
│  import pystan                                                               │
│                                                                              │
│  def main():                                                                 │
│      # 1) Load data                                                          │
│      train_X = pd.read_csv(&#x27;/app/train_X.csv&#x27;)                     │
│      train_y = pd.read_csv(&#x27;/app/train_y.csv&#x27;,                     │
│  header=None).squeeze().to_numpy()                                           │
│      test_X = pd.read_csv(&#x27;/app/test_X.csv&#x27;)                       │
│      with open(&#x27;/app/meta_public.json&#x27;, &#x27;r&#x27;) as f:       │
│          meta_public = json.load(f)                                          │
│                                                                              │
│      N = train_X.shape[0]                                                    │
│      D = train_X.shape[1]                                                    │
│      N_new = test_X.shape[0] if test_X is not None else 0                    │
│                                                                              │
│      P = int(meta_public.get(&#x27;P&#x27;, min(3, D)))                      │
│      jitter = float(meta_public.get(&#x27;jitter&#x27;, 1e-6))               │
│                                                                              │
│      # 2) Prepare data for Stan                                              │
│      # X: N x P (use first P features)                                       │
│      P_eff = min(P, D)                                                       │
│      X = train_X.iloc[:, :P_eff].to_numpy()                                  │
│                                                                              │
│      # H: intercept + first two features -&gt; shape (N, 3)                  │
│      H = np.ones((N, 3))                                                     │
│      if P_eff &gt;= 1:                                                       │
│          H[:, 1] = X[:, 0]                                                   │
│      if P_eff &gt;= 2:                                                       │
│          H[:, 2] = X[:, 1]                                                   │
│                                                                              │
│      y = train_y  # shape (N,)                                               │
│                                                                              │
│      # Stan model string (ARD squared-exponential GP with jitter on          │
│  diagonal)                                                                   │
│      stan_code = &quot;&quot;&quot;                                          │
│      data {                                                                  │
│          int&lt;lower=1&gt; N;              // number of data points         │
│          int&lt;lower=1&gt; P;              // kernel input dimension (ARD   │
│  features)                                                                   │
│          matrix[N, P] X;                // input features for kernel         │
│          matrix[N, 3] H;                // mean function design matrix       │
│          vector[N] y;                   // observations                      │
│          real jitter;                   // jitter on the diagonal            │
│      }                                                                       │
│      parameters {                                                            │
│          real&lt;lower=0&gt; alpha;                                          │
│          vector&lt;lower=0&gt;[P] rho;                                       │
│          vector[3] beta;                                                     │
│          real&lt;lower=0&gt; sigma;                                          │
│      }                                                                       │
│      transformed parameters {                                                │
│          matrix[N, N] K;                                                     │
│          matrix[N, N] Ky;                                                    │
│          // ARD squared-exponential kernel                                   │
│          for (i in 1:N) {                                                    │
│              for (j in 1:N) {                                                │
│                  real dist = 0;                                              │
│                  for (p in 1:P) {                                            │
│                      real diff = X[i, p] - X[j, p];                          │
│                      dist += rho[p] * diff * diff;                           │
│                  }                                                           │
│                  K[i, j] = alpha * exp(-0.5 * dist);                         │
│              }                                                               │
│          }                                                                   │
│          // Add white noise on diagonal: sigma^2 and jitter                  │
│          Ky = K;                                                             │
│          for (n in 1:N) {                                                    │
│              Ky[n, n] = Ky[n, n] + sigma * sigma + jitter;                   │
│          }                                                                   │
│      }                                                                       │
│      model {                                                                 │
│          // Priors (mirroring R script semantics)                            │
│          alpha ~ lognormal(0, 1);                                            │
│          rho ~ lognormal(0, 1);                                              │
│          beta ~ normal(0, 5);                                                │
│          sigma ~ cauchy(0, 2);                                               │
│                                                                              │
│          // Likelihood                                                       │
│          y ~ multi_normal(H * beta, Ky);                                     │
│      }                                                                       │
│      &quot;&quot;&quot;                                                      │
│                                                                              │
│      # 3) Compile and sample                                                 │
│      model = pystan.StanModel(model_code=stan_code)                          │
│      data = {                                                                │
│          &#x27;N&#x27;: N,                                                   │
│          &#x27;P&#x27;: P_eff,                                               │
│          &#x27;X&#x27;: X,                                                   │
│          &#x27;H&#x27;: H,                                                   │
│          &#x27;y&#x27;: y,                                                   │
│          &#x27;jitter&#x27;: jitter                                          │
│      }                                                                       │
│                                                                              │
│      control = {                                                             │
│          &#x27;adapt_delta&#x27;: 0.95,                                      │
│          &#x27;max_treedepth&#x27;: 12                                       │
│      }                                                                       │
│                                                                              │
│      fit = model.sampling(data=data, iter=2000, warmup=1000, chains=4,       │
│  seed=1, control=control)                                                    │
│                                                                              │
│      # 4) Extract posteriors and compute means                               │
│      extract = fit.extract(permuted=True)                                    │
│                                                                              │
│      alpha_post = float(np.mean(extract[&#x27;alpha&#x27;]))                 │
│      sigma_post = float(np.mean(extract[&#x27;sigma&#x27;]))                 │
│      rho_post = np.mean(extract[&#x27;rho&#x27;], axis=0)  # length P_eff    │
│      beta_post = np.mean(extract[&#x27;beta&#x27;], axis=0)  # length 3      │
│                                                                              │
│      # 5) Save numeric CSVs (no headers)                                     │
│      pd.Series([alpha_post]).to_csv(&#x27;/app/alpha_est.csv&#x27;,          │
│  index=False, header=False)                                                  │
│      pd.Series([sigma_post]).to_csv(&#x27;/app/sigma_est.csv&#x27;,          │
│  index=False, header=False)                                                  │
│      pd.Series(rho_post).to_csv(&#x27;/app/rho_est.csv&#x27;, index=False,   │
│  header=False)                                                               │
│      pd.Series(beta_post).to_csv(&#x27;/app/beta_est.csv&#x27;,              │
│  index=False, header=False)                                                  │
│                                                                              │
│  if __name__ == &quot;__main__&quot;:                                        │
│      main()                                                                  │
